deferred leaves and removed the rows of the SSverb_manager and SSinput stubs, now translated; checked against the code at the end of
wave 5, at the M1 integration, which added `level_trait`, and again at the M2 integration, which added the rows of the M2
packages and testers (P1, P2, P3, T1, T2 and the integration's own), removed the rows of the leaves M2 translated (`init_blood_types`,
the species lists, the nutrition update, space's air, `create_gas_mixture`, the login HUD) and of the SSmobs and SSpriority_effects
stubs (translated by M2 P1); M2 F1 rows added (the outfit's slots, clothing, the job outfit, the names) and the SSwardrobe stub, the `update_visible_name` row removed (translated) (every `Dm.Deferred` site in `src/Core/Tg` has a row and every row has its site); on 2026-10-13 the rows of the 224 hand-written sites that had none were added, grouped with their modules (the generated sites, the stubs of `Generated/LivePartial/**` and the inert types' initializers in `Generated/Types/**`, are covered by the two `generated:` rows, and the inert guard's refusals are not deferred leaves, DV27). The list that `docs/ai/translation-rules.md` 1.4 and 13 point to. Ordinary
`Dm.NotTranslated` sites are not listed here (every skeleton has them, and a test fails loudly when it reaches one); this
file lists what deliberately does not throw:

1. **Deferred leaves**: every `Dm.Deferred(where, what)` call (rules 1.4; lead decision W3 G1). A deferred leaf is allowed
   only when nothing in the current milestone reads its effects; it never throws, counts per world
   (`DmWorldState.DeferredCount`, `DeferredSites`) and logs its first call per site to the host log.
2. **Inert stub subsystems** (lead decision W3 G2): a subsystem type that exists with tg's `[DmPath]`, `name`,
   `init_stage` and `dependencies` but `ss_flags = SS_NO_INIT | SS_NO_FIRE`, so that `Master/New` creates it and the
   dependency graph resolves, until its milestone translates it.
3. **Seams** (rules 13): tg code that is never translated (TGS, rust-g, SQL, ...) and is replaced by a port seam.

A package that adds a deferred leaf, a stub or a seam reports it with the row's content; the lead adds the rows at the merge
(`docs/ai/specs/wave3-ownership.md`). A row is removed when the leaf, the stub or the seam is translated.

## 4. The M4a row plan (the M4a contracts step, 2026-10-09)

The 62 rows that name M4a today, by the package that removes them (it translates the leaf) or moves them to a later
milestone (with the lead), and the rows the M4a packages are expected to add. The owners are `docs/ai/specs/m4a-ownership.md`'s;
the lead edits this file at each merge from the packages' reports.

| Rows that name M4a now | Package | What happens |
|---|---|---|
| `code/datums/outfit.dm` (15: the deferred slots, the ID's trim and age, the accessory, the items in hands, the backpack and belt contents), `code/modules/jobs/job_types/_job.dm` (1: `post_equip`'s ID and PDA) | P5 | removed (translated); the bank account (SSeconomy, M10) and the PDA imprint (modular computers, after the core version) get rows of their own |
| `code/_onclick/hud/hud.dm` (8), `code/_onclick/hud/human.dm` (5), `code/_onclick/hud/living.dm` (1), `code/modules/mob/inventory.dm` (1), `code/game/objects/items.dm` (1), `code/game/atoms_movable.dm` (1, the throw path), `code/modules/mob/living/carbon/carbon_defense.dm` (1), `code/modules/mob/living/carbon/carbon.dm` (1), `code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/human.dm` (2), `code/modules/clothing/clothing.dm` (1), `code/modules/clothing/under/_under.dm` (1), `code/modules/surgery/bodyparts/default_parts.dm` (1), `code/game/objects/items/devices/scanners/health_analyzer.dm` (1) | P2 | removed where M4a's hands, slots, HUD and throwing translate them; a row whose leaf turns out to be another milestone's is moved to it (reported) |
| `code/game/objects/structures/tables_racks.dm` (4), `code/game/machinery/doors/{door,airlock,poddoor}.dm` (1, 1, 2), `code/game/machinery/_machinery.dm` (1), `code/datums/wires/_wires.dm` (1), `code/game/atom/atom_materials.dm` (1, `initialize_materials`: M5's materials, moved to M5 unless M4a's tools read it), `code/game/objects/structures/{window,railings}.dm` (1, 2), `code/game/objects/effects/spawners/random/structure.dm` (2), `code/game/objects/effects/decals/decal.dm` (1) | P4 | removed where the tool acts, the table placement and the pod doors' button path translate them; the wires UI rows move to M5/M7 (hacking, ROADMAP M4) |
| `code/modules/mob/living/carbon/human/_species.dm` (2), `SSwardrobe`, `SSgreyscale` (the stubs) | I1 | checked at the integration: moved to their real milestone or translated if an M4a path reads them |

**Rows the M4a packages are expected to add** (each reports them with its result): P1 the screentip icons
(`prepare_screentip_context_icons`), `set_hover_maptext`, the loot panel and the tgui `interact` blocks; P2 the item sounds
(M4b's audio), the item outline, `do_attack_animation`'s leftovers, the viewers of `visible_message` (M9), `stat_entry`;
P3 the storage's item actions and collection (M9), EMP (M7); P4 `handle_reactions` (M8), the welder's igniting and fire
(M4b); P5 SSid_access's TGUI lists and descriptions (M9), the bank account (M10), the PDA imprint, `apply_fingerprints`
(M9). The guard's refusals are not deferred leaves (DV27); they are counted with them for the soak.

## 5. Chemistry left out by the fire extinguisher (M4b, 2026-10-15)

The fire extinguisher (`game/objects/items/tools/extinguisher.cs`) brought in the part of the reagents code its spray
needs: the holder's `trans_to`, `copy_data`, `has_reagent`, `clear_reagents`, `expose` and `expose_temperature`; the
reagent base's `expose_atom`, `expose_mob`, `expose_obj`, `expose_turf` and `intercept_reagents_transfer`; the atom, turf,
obj, living and human `expose_reagents`; the exposure procs of water, firefighting foam and welding fuel; the atom, item and
glove `wash`; the reagent dispenser base. The rest of chemistry stays with M8 (the owner's tag for chemistry). Listed here at
the owner's request (2026-10-15), although most are ordinary `Dm.NotTranslated` sites that throw in tests:

`animate()` of a filter, tg's filter procs (`datums/datum.dm:334-528`) and filter makers (`__HELPERS/filters.dm`, but for the admin
editor's `GLOB.master_filter_info`), and the `/icon`'s `Blend` with `fcopy_rsc` (`DmShim/icon.cs`). Protocol version 25 carries an
appearance's filters and its render target (protocol.md section 34); the client draws the alpha mask, the outline, the displacement
map and the colour matrix (`AppearanceCompositor.Filters.cs`) and composes an `/icon`'s Blend recipe (`IconOps.cs`). Their callers in
M6: the one-handed glove's mask, the cranial fissure's displacement, the health doll's wound outline, the clickable alert's outline,
the damaged clothing's picture, the high gravity blur. M6 slice 7b (D156, protocol version 26): every other filter type and filter
animation reach the client too, and the rows that waited for filters are translated (an atom's colour filter on its overlays, a human's
height filters, the blood stain cut to a colour-filtered item, a damaged brain's grey, the canister's gas window, a coloured chair's
armrest, the leg's masked images). The leaves left:

| tg site | What | Reached by | Milestone | Port file |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| (client) | the filters of plane masters other than the game plane's gaussian blur (tg's `add_filter` on a plane or its controller: a drug's colour or wave, the lighting's rays; the non-master and colourblind controllers): the 3D client draws no planes; since protocol version 29 the game plane master controller's filters reach the client (protocol.md section 38) and it draws their blurs over its view (the eye blur), not the other kinds | a plane master's filter | with the client's post-processing (M9) | `godot/scripts/Render/ViewBlurLayer.cs` |
| (client) | an animated filter of a floor or wall decal (the map's batched decals keep one picture; only standing pictures, items and resting things are handed to the entity layer to animate) | no ported code animates one | with its first caller | `godot/scripts/MapRenderer.cs` |
| `code/__HELPERS/filters.dm:4` | `GLOB.master_filter_info`, the filter editor's data (filterrific) | the admin filter editor | M10 (admin tooling) | `__HELPERS/filters.cs` |
| (shim) | the `/icon` procs other than `Blend`, `DrawBox`, `MapColors`, `SetIntensity`, `Crop`, `Scale`, `Shift` and `SwapColor` (`Insert`, `GetPixel`, ...) | their callers | with their callers | `src/Core/DmShim/icon.cs` |

plane masters a seam: section 3; protocol version 29 draws the game plane's blur over the client's view), the brain trauma's base
(`brain_damage/brain_trauma.dm`), the concussion (`brain_damage/mild.dm:93`), the brain's trauma procs that gaining one runs and the

self-grasp hand item, and the hand item's own `Initialize` and `attack`), reached by tg's ctrl-click on oneself, which the client

uncalled), so the window's layer buttons stand for it.

(`_onclick/drag_drop.dm:129`, `:143`, `:173`: the row of section 6 is closed); an aimed shot's 3D line, its crossing with a carbon's drawn
body parts, its floor and ceiling and tg's spread as a cone are DV94 to DV97 (`ShotGeometry.cs`, `projectile.cs`). Leaves:

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code/modules/projectiles/projectile.dm:623` (`can_hit_target`) | a non-carbon living mob (an animal, a bot, a cyborg) hit by an aimed shot keeps tg's 2D rule (density, direct target, prone auto-aim, lying skip): the port has no table of their pictures' opaque boxes, so a shot over a mouse still hits it | an aimed shot crossing a non-carbon mob's turf | M7x (after the station set) or with the mobs' 3D models | `Infrastructure/ShotGeometry.cs` (`Crossing.NotApplicable`) |
| `code/_onclick/drag_drop.dm:128`, `:141` | `mouse_down_icon` and `mouse_up_icon`: the mouse pointer icon a press and a release set (`mouse_pointer_icon`); the first-person client draws its own crosshair and shows no pointer icon (tg's full auto, translated by M7x slice D, sets the weapon pointer on the client the same way, and nothing draws it either) | a gun or a tool that sets them (tg's full auto's crosshair) | the HUD's cursor work | `_onclick/drag_drop.cs`, `datums/components/fullauto.cs` |
| (BYOND's mouse) | MouseDown, MouseDrag and MouseUp are sent for the left button only (the right button's press opens the client's radial, its tap is a click; the middle button clicks): tg's procs see no right or middle press | a right- or middle-button autoclick (none of the station set's guns) | when a mechanic needs it | `GameScene.Interaction.cs` |
| `code/modules/projectiles/projectile.dm:934` (`process_movement`) | an aimed shot whose line reaches the floor or the ceiling (DV97) and does not stop there (the turf was already hit) is deleted instead of bouncing with its slope turned over. A ricochet off a floor is not reachable: only walls carry `flags_ricochet` (`walls.dm:15`, `mineral_walls.dm:274`), so a bullet's `check_ricochet_flag` refuses every floor and the shot is deleted there as tg's bullet is (M7x slice C checked this) | never (a floor with a ricochet flag) | when a floor type gets `flags_ricochet` | `modules/projectiles/projectile.cs` |

(`jitteriness.dm`, also closing the shock's deferred jitter) and the convulsions (`debuffs.dm:793-819`), the mock client's
teardown (`mob.dm:55`). tg's snapshot has no `DEFIB_TIME_LIMIT`: the limit is the heart's decay while dead (2.5 *

the brain's own decay while dead (half `STANDARD_ORGAN_DECAY`), both already translated (`handle_organs`, `_organ.dm:160`).

## 77. M9's leftovers re-tagged (M9 slice H, D265)

At M9's close every `Dm.Deferred` and `Dm.NotTranslated` still labelled M9 (556 sites: 426 written by hand and 130 rows of
`live-partial.txt`) was re-tagged in place: `M9` became `M10+` (after the core version's M10, planned when M10 is done), `M9-M10`
became `M10` and the mixed legacy tags of M2 (`not in M2 (M4a/M5/M9/M4b by area)` and the like) keep their other milestones with
`M10+` in M9's place. The sweep found five sites inside M9's scope and translated them: the shove's knockdown and side-kick lines to the
bystanders, the shover and the target, and a disarmed item's drop line (`living_defense.dm:851`, `:864`, `:879`); the bystanders' "puts

## 83. Admin tools (M10 slice I, D275)

Translated: `admin_ranks.dm` (the ranks and their keywords, `load_admin_ranks`, `ranks_from_rank_name`, `join_admin_ranks`, `load_admins`
from the text files, DV193); `holder2.dm` (the holder's creation, activation, association and rights, `check_rights`, `check_rights_for`,
`GenerateToken`); `client/New`'s admin association, the autoadmin and localhost ranks, and `client/Destroy`'s; `auto_deadmin` and the job's
and lobby's auto deadmin; the ticker's `Reboot` rights; `SSdbcore`'s round row through the host (`RoundDatabaseSeam`); `rights2text`,
`log_suspicious_login`. The host (rules 13: SQL) runs tg's `world/IsBanned`, `create_ban`, `unban`, `notify_all_banned_players`,
`create_message`, `delete_message`, `display_unread_notes`, `scream_about_watchlists` and the player row (ServerHost.Admin.cs, DV194,
DV195).

| the database's admin tables, their sync and backup, the 2FA of admin connections | not ported (DV193) | `admin_ranks.cs`, `holder2.cs` |
| role bans, sticky bans, the panic bunker, the whitelist, the population cap, the computer id, the related accounts and the player's age | not ported (DV194) | `sql_ban_system.cs`, `client_procs.cs` |
| the talk logs (log_talk), the blackbox statistics, the round's data and log files, the TGS relays | M10+ | their sites |

## 84. M10's leftovers re-tagged (M10 slice K)

At M10's close, every `Dm.Deferred` and `Dm.NotTranslated` still labelled M10 was re-tagged in place to `M10+` (after the core
version). That is 364 sites written by hand and 135 rows of `live-partial.txt`. Mixed labels (`M2-M10`, `M10 slice G4`) also became
`M10+`. The sweep found seven sites inside M10's scope and translated them:
- three that the shuttles needed: a shuttle's airlocks, blast doors and buttons take the shuttle's id into theirs (`connect_to_shuttle`:
  `airlock.dm:212`, `poddoor.dm:213`, `buttons.dm:81`);
- two parts of the supermatter cascade that waited for SSshuttle: the stranded emergency shuttle and the escaping one's backup route
  (`delamination_effects.dm:117`, `:208`);
- the reboot HUD for a player who logs in after the round's end (`login.dm:147`);
- the station clock's time of death (`death.dm:234`, `status_procs.dm:599`); the health analyzer's time stamp uses the station clock too.

What is left, by topic (about the number of sites):

| Topic | Sites | Milestone | Where |
|---|---|---|---|
| admin tooling: view variables and map saving, the admin verbs and panels, debug items | 226 | M10+ (section 83: the console commands stand in) | the machines' and items' files, `Generated/LivePartial/*.g.cs` |
| suicides (the suicide verb, `suicide_act`) | 44 | M10+ | the items' files |
| ghosts beyond D151's scope (orbit, the spawners menu, observing through eyes, dead chat notices) | 41 | M10+ (D151) | `observer.cs`, `ghost.cs`, `client_procs.cs` |
| the economy: bank accounts, payments, cargo orders and exports, the mail | 32 | translated (gaps P4a and P4b, sections 167 and 168) | `cards_ids.cs`, `_job.cs`, `id_access.cs`, `supply.cs` |
| antagonists, objectives, the uplink, the blob, the wizard | 27 | the alpha (ROADMAP section 9) | their sites |
| fantasy affixes | 16 | M10+ | the items' rows |
| station life: holidays, station traits, the round-start command report (the RETA grants translated, gaps SC1) | 7 | M10+ (M9x, D249; the threat report with the antagonists) | `priority_announce.cs`, `events.cs`, `station.cs`, `job.cs`, `communications` rows |
| round statistics, achievements, medals, persistence, playtime | 16 | M10+ | `blackbox.cs`, `supermatter.cs`, `_job.cs` |
| moods, memories, the honorifics, the lag switch (the skills came with gaps SL1, section 221) | 11 | M10+ | `living.cs`, `death.cs`, `observer.cs` |
| shuttle consoles and navigation computers, shuttle construction, the goggles' shuttle view | 8 | M10+ (DV188-DV190) | `airlock.cs`, `poddoor.cs`, `_open.cs`, `engine_goggles.cs` |

the client's `Crystallizer` and `Electrolyzer` windows and tgui's `getGasColor`. Both machines' `add_context` are `live-partial.txt`
rows (E12a: the screentips, not now by the owner, D264). The crystallizer's other products are made with tg's name, sprite and
quality prefix but stay inert until their own packages (their Initialize and own procs do not run, D112) (the healium, proto-nitrate,
nitrous oxide and foam crystals are live since gaps F1, `grenades/atmos_grenades.dm`): the metal hydrogen, diamond and zaukerite sheets
(`mineral.dm`: no crafting recipes), hot ice (`hot_ice.dm`), the ammonia crystals (`ammonia_crystals.dm`), the crystal cell
(`power/cell.dm:248`: its charge is not set up). The plasma sheet, the supermatter shard and the fuel pellets
(`explorer_drone/exodrone.dm`, live with the exploration drones, section 249) are live. Nothing else is left.

## 121. The cybernetic implants (P6, D298)

Translated: `cyberimp/augments_arms.dm`, `augments_chest.dm` and `augments_internal.dm` whole (with P1's part of the last): the arm implants'
hand link, the toolkits with their button, radial, drop key, swap on use and EMP (the engineering, surgical, hacked and morbid surgical,
paperwork, laser, taser, energy blade, medical beamgun, flash, baton and combat kits), the Strong-Arm implant with its punch and its two-arm
set bonus (`datums/elements/organ_set_bonus.dm` whole: the element and the status effect), the nutriment pumps, the reviver, the implantable
thrusters (the jetpack component on the implant, with the locomotion bridge's preview), the Herculean and Atlas spines, the anti-drop
implant, the CNS rebooter, the skillchip connector and the breathing tube; the organ toggle and use actions' names (`organ_action.dm`), the
suspicious autosurgeons (`autosurgeon.dm:155-212`), the photon projector (`flash.dm:339-372`), the electrically-charged arm
(`robot/items/generic.dm:19-60`), the mounted laser and taser (`guns/energy/mounted.dm` whole), the MOD electronic stamp
(`mod/modules/modules_maint.dm:293-315`), the energy blade with its hardlight kind (`weaponry/melee/energy.dm`, whole with the syndicate campaign G2, section 135),
the medical beamgun (`guns/special/medbeam.dm` whole) with `los_check` (`__HELPERS/atoms.dm:375`), the human's `limb_attack_self` (the empty hand's use opens an arm kit's radial), the carbon's
`getPlasma` and `adjustPlasma` (`alien/adult/alien_powers.dm:381-395`). tg's own quirks kept: the anti-drop implant's EMP releases the items
before its throw loop, so nothing is thrown; the connector passes the slot name `ORGAN_SLOT_BRAIN` to `get_organ_by_type`, so its EMP and its
failing use find no brain and only play the slap; `insert_skillchip` shows `fail_string` for an activation failure; the energy blade's spark system is made with `new(5, FALSE, src)`, so it has
no sparks (its cardinals flag, a number here, is TRUE where tg stores src); `los_check` leaves its dummy on a turf whose border blocks the
first step. The leaves and what stays
inert:

tg has it: the monkey agent gets no objective (tg adds the antagonist before the beacon binds the mind to its buyer) and its allergy
never fires (the beacon that registers it deletes itself at once); His Grace's stun message reads " Grace protects you" (tg applies
the absorption before it sets His word). Adapted: the medical wounds unit test keeps its victim's limbs (the boot's mutation shuffle
moved the seed onto the critical tier's outright dismemberment). Stage G4d (the sentry gun with the portable turrets) comes next; until
then the turret toolbox's entry stays hidden (DV343).

flat on it with a steady outline (DV383). Two engine fixes came with it:
the server's replicator re-reads the vis_contents holders of an atom whose look changed (BYOND redraws an atom's vis_locs; the feed and
its copies are in nullspace), from an index of its own (the world's vis_locs index fills lazily and is world state), and a vis_contents
child with `VIS_INHERIT_LAYER` or `VIS_INHERIT_PLANE` is flattened at `FLOAT_LAYER` and `FLOAT_PLANE` (on its own layer the feed went
under the display's screen). The shim's `new /mutable_appearance(source)` and `appearance =` now copy the source's filters, as BYOND's
do (the copy of a mob keeps its glow or outline). Nothing of the greenscreen is left.

Translated (S4a): Plexagon Access Management (`file_system/programs/card.dm` whole: the two ID slots, logging in with a card that has
`ACCESS_CHANGE_IDS` or, limited to their departments, a head's card, the job templates, the access and wildcard toggles, renaming, the
age, the assignment, demoting and the access report), Plexagon HR Core (`jobmanagement.dm` whole: the slots against the crew's size and
the config's cooldown, the prioritized jobs), the Medical and Security Records (`records.dm` whole) and the status display app
(`statusdisplay.dm:1-91`); the client's NtosCard (with the agent card's common AccessList), NtosJobManager, NtosRecords and NtosStatus
(the communications console's StatusDisplayControls) windows. tg's rules kept: a template clears the card and adds the job's access as
far as the card's trim allows (`add_trim_access_to_card`), and no slot opens while the crew is under the 30 % ratio. The status app's
radio signal names its computer as the source (tg names the program, a datum; the port's signal source is an obj).

Translated (S4b): AmpCheck (`powermonitor.dm` whole, with the power monitoring console's PowerMonitor content), SignalCommander (`signalcommander.dm:1-98`), BotKeeper (`robocontrol.dm` whole, with `GLOB.drone_machine_blacklist_enabled` of `__DEFINES/drone.dm`), SiliConnect and Roboverlord (`borg_monitor.dm:1-188`), WireCarp (`wirecarp.dm:1-69`), the NTNRC chat client and its channels (`chatroom/ntnrc_client.dm` and `conversation.dm` whole) with `reject_bad_chattext` (`__HELPERS/text.dm:1166`); the client's NtosPowerMonitor, NtosSignaler, NtosRoboControl, NtosCyborgRemoteMonitor, NtosNetMonitor and NtosNetChat windows. BotKeeper's ping sound plays at its computer (tg plays it at the program, a datum).

Translated (S4c): EmojiPedia (`emojipedia.dm`), the Physical Scanner (`maintenance/phys_scanner.dm`), the Botanical Encyclopedia (`botanical_encyclopedia.dm`), the Donksoft Micro Arcade and its maintenance kind (`arcade.dm`, with the living's `won_game`, `lost_game` and `played_game`, `living.dm:2887-2900`), the NtOS Cursor Replacer (`maintenance/cool_sword.dm`), the PDA themes (`maintenance/themes.dm`), the Spectre-Meter (`maintenance/spectre_meter.dm:1-138`), the DoS Traffic Generator (`antagonist/dos.dm`) and Revelation (`antagonist/revelation.dm`); the client's NtosEmojipedia (a click copies the emoji's tag through the client's clipboard), NtosPhysScanner, NtosBotanicalEncyclopedia, NtosCursor, NtosNetDos, NtosRevelation, NtosArcade (tg's boss GIFs, converted by the icon tool) and NtosSpectreMeter windows. tg's own quirk kept as its runtime leaves it: Revelation's purge deletes itself with the computer's files (computer_file/Destroy nulls its computer), so the damage and the battery's explosion after it never happen. Left out:

Translated (S4d): the Virtual Pet (`virtual_pet.dm` whole but its photo: the Orbie made at install and asleep in the PDA, released and recalled, fed with the virtual chocolate dropped at a random area, hunger and happiness, levels from steps and play, the hats, colours, name and gender, the trick sequence and the shared feed of every pet's milestones with their likes) and its Orbie (`pets/orbie/orbie.dm`, `orbie_abilities.dm`, `orbie_ai.dm` whole); the client's NtosVirtualPet window, its pictures drawn from their parts (DV405). tg's own quirk kept: the pet colour the window reads back (`pet_color`) is looked up from the pet's `atom_colours[FIXED_COLOUR_PRIORITY]`, which holds `list(colour, type)`, so it is never found and the window shows no colour chosen. The hats locked behind achievements are offered at their level, as in tg while the achievements subsystem is not running (`SSachievements` is not ported).

Translated (S4d, Mafia): `modules/mafia` whole (the controller with its phases, timers, votes, night curtains and red popup, its panel's data and actions, the roles, the abilities, the game board and the map templates, the obsessed's outfit), `mafia_ntos.dm`, the ghost's Minigames menu (`datums/minigames_menu.dm`, with its HUD button and verb, `screen_objects/ghost.dm:65-72`, `observer.dm:973-982`), the temporary body (`datums/components/temporary_body.dm`), the shadowpeople (`species_types/shadowpeople.dm`, the nightmare's reveal) and the map generator modules (`procedural_mapping/mapGeneratorModule.dm`, `mapGeneratorModules/helpers.dm` and the rest of `mapGenerator.dm`: the mass delete that clears the game's map); tg's seven Mafia maps (`_maps/minigame/mafia`) converted and mounted at tg's paths; the client's MafiaPanel, NtosMafiaPanel and MinigamesMenu windows. tg's own quirks kept: the timers never speed up (`start_day` checks `!time_speedup`, which starts at 1); a dead-chat message stops at the first living role by day (`send_message`'s `return`); a security officer guarding itself does not stop the kill (`defend`'s `return FALSE`); a body change unregisters the new body's speech (`register_body`); the panel's theme is never sent (`roleinfo` has no `role_theme`); tg's MafiaPanel shows each player's `votes`, which the server never sends. The ghost's windows close when it goes into its body (`mob/Logout`): the body's Mafia Panel action reopens the panel. The red popup's width is the world's view (`client.view_size` is not declared; the port's HUD lays out tg's 15 x 15 view, D107).

communications blackout, the processor overload, the gravity generator blackout (and the high gravitational anomaly's), the supermatter

`SSjob`'s `get_valid_overflow_jobs` and `has_minimum_jobs`. Two tg slips fixed: the market crash's `tick_counter = tick_counter++`
never counts, so the vendors were never repriced during the crash (its comment wants every fifth tick); the bureaucratic error took its
jobs out of the static list of `get_valid_overflow_jobs` itself, so each run shrank the next one's pool (the port takes from a copy).
DEVIATION(DV380): the false alarm leaves out the midround rulesets that are not ported (inert, DV346).

## 184. The station traits (gaps R2, D327)

Translated: `datums/station_traits/*.dm` whole (the trait datum with its round start hook, code trait, report line and lobby button;
the positive, negative, neutral and job traits; the repair technician's ID, the birthday invitation and the party hats; the admins'
Modify Station Traits window, opened by the admin console's `station-traits`, D275), SSstation's traits (`SetupTraits`, `pick_traits`,
`setup_trait`, `Recover`, the future traits file, the processing of the traits that process), three of the five station trait jobs
(`jobs/job_types/station_trait/cargo_gorilla.dm`, `bridge_assistant.dm`, `pun_pun.dm`: the cargorilla spawns at the gorilla's start
with his account and ID, the bridge assistant on the bridge, a chair first, Pun Pun as the bar's monkey made a crewman), and the
readers that waited for them: the round-start report's trait lines, the lobby's sign-up buttons (`lobby_menu.dm` `get_station_traits`
and `sign_up`, and the client's lobby screen), the random arrivals' hallway spawn (`_job.dm:479`), the hangover spawn whole
(`landmarks.dm:514-606`), the assistants' gimmick loadouts (`assistant.dm:41`, `gimmick_assistants.dm`), the wallets (`wallets.dm`
whole, the wallet storage, the ID card's `update_in_wallet`), the skub packs (`service_boxes.dm:257-278`,
`storage/subtypes/boxes.dm:150-163`), the gravity generator's nebula shielding (`gravitygenerator.dm:169`, `add_to_nebula_shielding`),
the job department's `remove_job`, and the postal strike's mail strike loan (the shuttle loans came with gaps E3). The radioactive
nebula's decal colour (`get_decal_color`) is read by the holidays' `request_decoration_colors` (`__HELPERS/events.dm:96`, gaps R3), and
monkey day sets up Pun Pun is a Crewmember (`holidays.dm:857`). The icemoon's bright day and forested traits are planetary (`STATION_TRAIT_PLANETARY`): the space station never
rolls them, and their readers (`areas/mining.dm:216`, `:230`, `:267`) wait with the icemoon's areas. DEVIATION(DV432): SSstation
leaves out of the random pool a trait whose whole effect is a system not ported yet (`TraitsWaitingForTheirSystem` in `station.cs`,
each with the type of that system: the trait enters the pool by itself when the type turns live) and, as SSevents does (DV380), a
random event modifier whose event is not ported; an admin may still force any of them (the panel, the future traits file). A world
booted without tg's configuration file (the toy and test worlds) forbids the traits (`TgWorld.Boot`), as tg's unit test build skips
them; the server rolls them with `STATION_TRAITS` (on in the shipped `config/server.txt`) and carries the future traits file over a
reboot.

## 205. The assemblies (gaps AS1, D333)
Translated whole: `modules/assembly/holder.dm`, `timer.dm` but its suicide, `health.dm`, `infrared.dm`, `mousetrap.dm`, `voice.dm`,
with their Timer, HealthSensor and InfraredEmitter windows; `assembly.dm` finished (the holder's branches, `ui_host` and `ui_state`,
which were live-partial stubs); `datums/components/electrified_buckle.dm` and `game/objects/structures/electricchair.dm` whole, with
the chairs' and the sofa's `electrify_self` and the tram's electrified rail; the holder's hosts: gibtonite's rig
(`mining/ores_coins.dm`), the rigged fuel tank (`reagent_dispenser.dm`: `IsSpecialAssembly`, `rig_boom`, the rig and its removal),
the cup's lid (`cups/_cup.dm`: `attach_assembly`, `on_igniter_activate`), the tank bomb (`tanks/tanks.dm`: `IsSpecialAssembly`,
`receive_signal`, `bomb_assemble`, `ignite`); the explosive wires' health sensor (`datums/wires/explosive.dm:66`). The beep lines'
chat icons (`icon2html`) of the timer, health sensor and infrared emitter are left out as at every other such site (section 76).
GAP(types): an assembly wired to a door or an APC keeps `holder` null (the port types it an item; tg stores the host there, typed
the holder, and nothing reads it through that type); gibtonite's `rig_overlay` and the dispenser's `assembliesoverlay` are typed
mutable appearances, so the holder itself is the overlay, as tg's `add_overlay` takes it; the dispenser's `last_rigger` is typed a
string and keeps the rigger's key name; a rigged tank is not its holder's `master` (typed an item, `items.dm:108`; nothing a player
sees reads it on a tank).

Translated whole: `stacks/sheets/leather.dm` (a knife shaves a hide into a hairless hide, water soaks it into a wet hide that dries in
warm air, on a drying rack, in a microwave, on a grill or in an oven into leather; the lizard hide keeps its lizard's scale colour (a
random one when spawned), merges only by it and splits with it; the hide, leather, sinew and pelt recipes, sinew's trophy slapcraft and the ash drake
hide's armour plates), `wall_filling.dm` (the plastitanium wall and window fillings reskinned in hand, judged as throwing weapons on
examine and welded back into two sheets), `bscrystal.dm` (the bluespace polycrystal broken off from the inactive hand, its recipes),
`sheets/light.dm` (wired glass tiles made from iron), `sheets/hot_ice.dm`, `tickets.dm`, `ammonia_crystals.dm`, `tiles/tile_mineral.dm`
(four mineral tiles welded into a sheet); the procs left of `sheets/mineral.dm` (the recipe lists of diamond, gold, silver, bananium,
titanium, snow, adamantine, runite, mythril, alien alloy, metallic hydrogen and zaukerite; five rods on titanium make a shuttle frame;
snow hates storms; a welder lights coal into carbon dioxide), `sheets/sheet_types.dm` (the cloth, bamboo, durathread, cardboard with its
fancy boxes, bronze, pizza and hauntium recipes; durathread sewn into clothing armour; the bone's slapcraft; a clown stamp turns

## 218. The items' odds, part 2 (gaps OD2, D333)
Translated whole: `items/v8_engine.dm` (the V8 engine held in both hands, its roar and shake in hand, its label teaching the House
Edge's crafting recipe on a close examine; the House Edge's fire charges from wielded hits on the living, drawn as "house_edge1" to
"house_edge3", lost when dropped, thrown or used in hand, and spent on a right-click blazing slash: the focus ring of its wind-up, a
flaming projectile hitting for ten more per charge, and a light blast with flames when the second wind-up completes at three
charges), `drug_items.dm` (the contraband saturnX glob, moon rock, crystal meth and opium with their picked pictures; the meth
crystal's embedding injecting its meth into an organic limb; the raw opium's morphine by its potency, pressed together up to ten
units; the bLaSToFF ampoule snapped open in hand, "_open" and "_empty", and shattered into a shard and a splash when thrown),
`flatpacks.dm` (a flatpack named after its machine, deployed on a free open floor with a multitool after a second with the mook dust
and the terminal's eject, its packed parts the machine's; the flatpack cart's three stacked "flatcart_flat" packs, the topmost handed
back), `piggy_bank.dm` (six greyscale colours, cash, holochips and coins up to the limit, the rattle by fullness, the shatter that
spills the money, the chips squashed into one past 35, the museum's Pigston Swinelord VI kept in its area, the vault's savings per
shift), `etherealdiscoball.dm` (deployed, switched on and off, a random colour and light every half second; as in tg the last colour
stays after it is switched off), `debug_items.dm` (the human spawner, its species from tgui's list prompt, DV532; the omnitool's
radial of every tool and its mode on examine; the spawntool, which spawns a typepath's types or subtypes for an admin and gibs a
player without admin rights who confirms twice), `busts_and_figurines.dm` (the busts' art and beauty; the Hippocrates bust's medical
HUD while held, the four-line Hippocratic Oath and its forswearing once per five minutes, the bruised brain of a slip, the Solemn Vow;
the Maneki-Neko's colour and paw overlay), `virgin_mary.dm` (burnt, a mafia nickname between the first and last names, tg's double
space kept; the Hail Mary suicide), `rollertable_dock.dm` (the later of tg's two `deploy_rolling_table`s, which keeps the dock, is the
one that runs; the earlier is `deploy_rolling_table__d0`), `botpad_remote.dm` with `game/machinery/botlaunchpad.dm` (the bot pad
linked from a multitool's buffer, its open panel "botpad-open", the launch of a lone bot in a see-through transport pod and its
recall), `dna_probe.dm` (the carp DNA sampler; the DNA sampler was gaps R1's), `maintenance_loot.dm` with
`modules/bitrunning/components/avatar_gear.dm` (the lead pipe's and the lead-acid battery's grinding, the battery 40-60 % charged and
fishing bait, the avatar gear component following its topmost human carrier), `dyespray.dm` (hair and facial hair gradients and the
dyeable external body parts through tgui's alert, list and colour prompts), `gun_maintenance.dm` (as in tg, a ballistic gun always
spends a use), `pitchfork.dm`; the holy skateboard's `Initialize` of `skateboards.dm`; `datums/components/areabound.dm`, the focus
ring of `effects/temporary_visuals/miscellaneous.dm` and `_globalvars/lists/engineering.dm` whole (`all_tool_behaviours` and

Translated whole: `reagents/drinks/alcohol_reagents.dm` (the ethanol base: the drunkenness a drink adds by its boozepwr, its volume and
the server's alcohol rate, the alcohol tolerance and the light drinker traits, water's dilution, the monkeys' anger, the liver's damage
by its tolerance and the heavy drinker trait; the ink it washes off paper and books; a splash's fire stacks and the sterilizing
surgery speed; the natural drinks' quality and boozepwr from the fermenting barrel; and the 230 alcohols' own effects: the job drinks
(the security officer's beepsky smash, quadruple and quintuple sec, the bartender's handshake, the clown's banana honk, the mime's
silencer, the engineer's screwdriver, the dwarves' manly dorf), the healing drinks (the hearty punch for the dying, the bastion
bourbon, the bilk, the barefoot without shoes), the Pan-Galactic Gargle Blaster by its cycle, the

Translated whole: `datums/mood.dm` (the mood datum every human makes in `setup_mood`, the dummy's; the mood events' add, refresh,

recovery, the crit threshold, the movespeed and actionspeed modifiers, the sanity hallucination and the insanity; the hunger moodlets
read from the nutrition; the area beauty with the area-sensitive trait; `print_mood` on the mood face's click; the mood face on the
HUD with tg's `mood1`-`mood9` and special states and the sanity colours; the revive, death and admin hooks),
`controllers/subsystem/moods.dm` (SSmood), every proc of `datums/mood_events/` (`_mood_event.dm` with the personality and job checks,
`death.dm`'s ten see-death moodlets, `generic_positive_events.dm`, `generic_negative_events.dm`, `drug_events.dm`, `drink_events.dm`,
`food_events.dm`, `needs_events.dm`, `area_events.dm`; the other files are var blocks), so every `/datum/mood_event` type is live,
with the mood events of other files (`aoe_moodlet.dm`'s ugly and pretty mutations, the pyromania and pyrophobia moods, the spacer's
low gravity mood, the clown world finale's mood), `__DEFINES/mood.dm`'s `HAS_PERSONALITY` and `IS_DESENSITIZED`,
`__HELPERS/mobs.dm`'s `add_personality_mood_to_viewers`, the living's `add_mood_event`, `clear_mood_event` and the admin's add and
remove mood dialogs, and the gonbola's pacify status effect (`debuffs.dm:663`, `handcuffs.dm:718`). Swept, the mood readers made
real: the stomach's hunger rate by sanity, the sleep's quality by sanity (`debuffs.dm:255`), the apathetic trait, the update of the
nutrition, the beauty element of the posters, the cleanables and the shiny and dull materials, the hug's friendly moods and heart,
the reattached limb's moods, the surgical processor's EMP insanity, the psy lens beam, the demoraliser, the brain damage moodlet, the
felinid's feral bite, sweet coffee and tea, psicodine, naloxone, sadness, the combat PTSD, the drunk moodlet's update, the hit
splatter's blood, the slip's and the revolution's personality viewers, the balloon mallet, the operations' mood reads and the
roughrider's riding speed (`riding_mob.dm:138`). Every other `add_mood_event` and `clear_mood_event` call of the port now makes or
clears its mood. Kept as tg has them: `print_mood` tests `TRAIT_NOHUNGER` on the mood datum (never true, so the hunger line is always
read); the helped-up moodlet's `can_effect_mob` reads its `owner` before `on_add` sets it, so it never lands (tg's runtime is not
reproduced); `add_personality_mood_to_viewers` passes its range on as the mood's first argument; the mallet, the psy lens beam,
sweet coffee and tea and sadness end where tg's unguarded `mob_mood` read would runtime on a moodless mob. The friendly hug of a

## 230. Bitrunning (gaps BR1-2, D333, D339, D341)
Gaps BR1 translated bitrunning's machinery whole: `modules/bitrunning/server/` (the quantum server: the domain lazily loaded into a
reservation from its converted map, its landmarks read into exits, the send turfs, the cache and the curiosities, the advanced NPCs'
poll, the threat with the glitch's spawn and the mutation of a domain mob, the rewards with the grade, the certificate and the tech disk,
the decrypted crate and curiosity printed by a nearby byteforge, the shutdown, the reset and the scrub, the broadcast, the emag, the
parts), `netpod/` (the pod's entry with its do_after and the hands blocked, the stasis healing, the outfits of the NetpodOutfits window,
the copy-body toggle, the disconnection's static vision, blindness, paralysis, flash, brain damage and scream, the auto-drain, the
tram door sounds, the opening and closing flicks and the states), `components/` (the avatar's connection with the hacker alias, the
copied body, the bit avatar's job, the skills copied to the avatar and back, the linked damage, the safe and forced disconnections and
the alerts; the netpod healing with its embryonic status effect; the npc friendly factions; the virtual entity; the glitch), `objects/`
(the quantum console and its QuantumConsole window, the byteforge with its particles and print flicker, the hololadder, the host
monitor, the encrypted and decrypted caches with the ore rewards, the landmarks, the ability and item disks, the gimmick disks with the
dungeon's alchemist bottles and the sports laptop's programs, the bitrunning order console paid in bitrunning points), `abilities.dm`
(the domain info action and its AvatarHelp window), `alerts.dm`, `areas.dm` (`GLOB.virtual_areas`, which NT Pay's virtual check
reads), `outfits.dm`, `spawners.dm`, `job.dm`, `turfs.dm`, `util/digital_aura.dm`, `service_style.dm`, `virtual_mob.dm`,
`virtual_domain/virtual_domain.dm`, `modular_mob_segment.dm`, the test domain, the gondola asteroid and the grasslands hunt, and
`controllers/subsystem/networks/bitrunning.dm` with `_globalvars/lists/bitrunning.dm`. With them: `mapping/modular_map_loader/
modular_map_loader.dm` (the safehouses on the domains' modular roots, read from `strings/modular_maps/safehouse.toml`),
`rustg_read_toml_file`, `status_effects/debuffs/static_vision.dm`, `holodeck/turfs.dm`, the baseturf helper (`mapping_helpers.dm:6-46`),
the dense rock's Initialize (`indestructible.dm:268`), the decrypted curiosity (`storage/lockbox.dm:324-354`), the infinite pizza box
(`pizzabox.dm:363-429`) and the bitrunning glitch's `fix_agent_id` (`antagonists/_parent.dm:79`). Every domain map and safehouse is
converted (`maps/virtual_domains`, `maps/safehouses`) and mounted at tg's `_maps/` paths (MapSeam). Kept as tg has it: the server's
`load_map_items` tests its bitrunning landmarks for a mob spawner (never one, so a domain's ghost spawners are only those the
advanced NPCs' load lists); `validate_turf` returns the turf it was given when a neighbour is open; the baseturf helper's
`list(baseturf_to_replace = TRUE)` keys the string; the beach bum's two spare rounds stay in nullspace when the pockets take the
others. Gaps BR2 translated the other domains and the glitch: `virtual_domain/domains/` (the beach bar's drinks and the breeze bay's
fish that reveal the crate, the island brawl's deaths counted from its islanders' spawners, the heretic hunt's bodies dragged onto
the rune and scored by rank with its flick, sound and balloon, the fake crewmen with their weapons, corpses and behaviour trees, the
virtual heretic's outfit, the wendigo domain's gutted corpses; the other domains are var-only with their maps), `antagonists/`
whole (the glitch's objective, virtual body, npc friendliness and agent look; the cyber police with the sleeping carp, the cyber
tactical unit with its implant, the netguardian prime with its burst SMG and rocket barrage, the domain ghost actor), `event.dm`
but the admins' setups, and the clients' AntagInfoGlitch window. With them the domains' mobs: `basic/revolutionary.dm`, the

## 231. The circuits' core and editor (gaps CI1, D333, D339, D341)
Translated whole: `modules/wiremod/core/` (`integrated_circuit.dm` with the circuit's cell, shell, display name, variables, setters and
getters, the IntegratedCircuit window's every act and the component printer link; `component.dm` with the ports, the triggers, the
power drawn from the cell per input, the instant execution, the UI notices and the USB parent hooks; `port.dm`; `datatypes.dm`;
`variable.dm`; `usb_cable.dm`; `marker.dm`, the circuit multitool with its marking flick, sound and overlay; `component_printer.dm`, the
component printer, the debug component printer and the module duplicator with their ComponentPrinter window, flicks and sounds;
`duplicator.dm`, the JSON save and load of a circuit; `program_circuit.dm`, the computer program component's base; `assets.dm`'s
component list (DV568); `admin_panel.dm`, View All Circuits as the console's `circuits` and Load Circuit as `load-circuit JSON`, D275),
`controllers/subsystem/processing/clock_component.dm`, `networks/circuit_component.dm` (SScircuit_component with the instant
execution) and `networks/wiremod_composite.dm` (the list and table datatypes), `__DEFINES/wiremod.dm`'s macros,
`_globalvars/lists/wiremod.dm`, `datums/components/shell.dm` (the shell component: the circuit put in and taken out, locking, the
capacity, the USB parent, the shell's power), `datums/wires/wire_bundle_component.dm`, `modules/wiremod/datatypes/` (every datatype
with the composite list, assoc list and table), `dcs_components/` (`component_add_port.dm`, `component_wirenet_connection.dm`), the
component categories that compute: `components/abstract/` (compare, variable, list and assoc list variables, the module component with
its CircuitModule window), `math/`, `list/` (with the format component's regex), `string/`, `table/`, `variables/`, `utility/` (the
clock, the delay, the NFC transmitters and receiver, the router and multiplexer, the timepiece, the typecast and typecheck, the wire
bundle), and the shells `shell/compact_remote.dm`, `controller.dm` and `shell_items.dm` (DV570). The readers swept: SignalCommander's
signal from a circuit (the creator and owner) and the uplink failsafe's circuit creator. Kept as tg has them: a DM default applies to
an explicit null, so `trigger = null` on a port still triggers `input_received`; the NFC list literal transmitter sends no sender, so
its receiver reads the data list as the sender; the wire bundle calls its parent's `removed_from` twice; tg's `circuit_component_category`
unit test loops over typepaths with a typed variable and so checks nothing (the port checks every design). The editor's adaptation is
DV571. Left:
| `wiremod/core/duplicator.dm:223` (`load_circuit`'s input) | tg's "File" option, and a JSON longer than one console line: the console's `load-circuit JSON` is a player's console line, capped at 200 bytes by the protocol (`Limits.MaxDevCommandBytes`), so only a small circuit is typed in | an admin loading a saved circuit | the admin tools | `src/Server/ServerHost.Console.cs` |
| `wiremod/core/integrated_circuit.dm:417`, `:680` (`isAdminGhostAI`) | an admin ghost or AI's admin view of a circuit (is_admin, printing any component): the port's admin ghost check is a GAP, false | an admin ghost | the admin tools | `modules/wiremod/core/integrated_circuit.cs` |

## 232. The circuits' components and shells (gaps CI2, D333, D339, D341)
Translated whole: the components of `modules/wiremod/components/action/` (camera, equipment action with its innate action, laser
pointer with its flash, light, MMI with its disconnect action, pathfind, pull, radio, sound emitter, speech), `atom/` (direction,
GPS, health, health state, hear, material scanner, pinpointer, reagent scanner, the remote cameras of the drone, the airlock and the
polaroid, self, species), `id/` (the access checker with its CircuitAccessChecker window, the access reader, the ID getter, the info
reader), `ntnet/` (receive, send, send literal), `sensors/` (pressure, temperature, view), `wirenet/` (receive, send, send literal; the
wirenet connection came with the core), `admin/` (animate, filter with the filter, flag and bitflag helpers, get and set variable,
input request, save shell, the signal handler with its CircuitSignalHandler window and signal list, spawn, to type);
`dcs_components/component_boris_circuit_container.dm` (an AI in a circuit's MMI component, with the AIs' HUD alternate appearance); the shells
`airlock.dm` (the circuit airlock, its wires and its airlock and access event components), `assembly.dm` (the wiremod assembly with its
input and output components and its power proxies), `bot.dm`, `dispenser.dm` (with the vendor component), `drone.dm` (the circuit drone
and its movement component), `gun.dm`, `keyboard.dm`, `module.dm` (the MOD circuit module with its pinned actions and adapter core),
`moneybot.dm`, `scanner.dm`, `scanner_gate.dm` (with its passive and scanning lines), `server.dm`, `undertile.dm`, `wallmount.dm`; every
shell assembly but the BCI's now finishes into its shell (DV570); `preset/hello_world.dm` and `speech_relay.dm` (Meta's four loaded
circuits in science). The research designs of these components and shells are offered (DV384) and the techweb's circuit nodes unlock
them. Dependencies ported with them: `datums/components/shuttle_move_deferred_checks.dm`, the AIs' basic alternate appearance
(`atom_hud/alternate_appearance/basic/ais`), `datumvars.dm`'s `vv_var_blacklist` and `can_vv_get`, `log_admin_circuit`,
`__HELPERS/filters.dm`'s `master_filter_info`. The readers swept: the airlock electronics' shell flag finishes a circuit airlock
(`door_assembly.dm:356`), the photo camera is a circuit shell (`camera.dm:71`), the research suit carries its circuit module, the IED
takes the wiremod assembly. A core fix: the NFC and NTNet receivers take the list literal transmitters' output port (tg types the
local as an input port). The remote camera is a station camera on its network, which the camera consoles show; the view sensor reads
the server's view; the laser pointer's dot is the flick overlay the handheld laser pointer shows: no new adaptation. Kept as tg has
them: the material scanner breaks alloys down; the dispenser's stored item that moves away is never removed from its list, and its
removal fires the "added" output; the MOD module's pinned action keeps its deleted pinner; `shuttle_move_deferred_checks` adds its
check with LAZYOR on a copy; the radio's frequency port starts at the signaler's frequency; an anchored wirenet shell with no cable
under it runtimes (the connection attaches connect_loc to itself); the speech relay preset leaves its speech's trigger unwired; the
equipment action's design has no category. The sound emitter's backwards playback is DV603. Left:

| `wiremod/components/admin/sdql.dm` | the SDQL component: an admin's circuit runs an SDQL2 query (SDQL2 is not ported) | an admin circuit | the admin tools (D275) | (inert) |

## 233. The brain-computer interface and the machines' USB components (gaps CI3, D333, D339, D341)
Translated whole: `wiremod/shell/brain_computer_interface.dm` (the BCI with its core component, its message to the owner, its charge
from a cyborg recharger or an electric shock and its charge action, the owner's speech through it; the BCI implanter with its occupant,
the BCI loaded and swapped, the arm raising, working and falling, the implant put in or taken back into storage, the ejection, the
lock and the deconstruction), `shell/implant.dm` (the circuit implant, its core and charge action; the implant case and its design hold
a live implant), `wiremod/components/bci/` (the install detector, reagent injector, thought listener, VOX, and `hud/`: the object, bar
and counter overlays, drawn as one-person alternate appearances that only the owner's client gets, and the target intercept),
`components/usb/` (the emitter, temperature pump and thermomachine components), the BCI's remote camera (`remotecam/bci` of
`components/atom/remotecam.dm`), `datums/components/usb_port.dm` (the cable plugged in, the machine's components added to the circuit,
the cable's beam to the shell, the snap past two tiles), `computer_circuit.dm` (a computer's shell with its programs' components) and
every machine's and program's own circuit component and USB port: the holographic signboard, the reflector, the vitals display, the
launchpad, the fire alarm, the status displays, the light switch, the crew, teleporter, security records and advanced camera consoles
(the latter's circuit action and intercept), the conveyor switch, the hydroponics tray, the synthesizer and headphones, the slime
processor, the emitter, the meter, the pumps, the digital valve, the temperature pump, the thermomachine, the air alarm
(`air_alarm_circuit.dm`); the status display, NT Pay, signal commander, messenger (`messenger_circuit.dm`), notepad, radar, WireCarp,
cyborg monitor, MODsuit control and spectre meter apps. With them: `censor_ic_filter_for_pdas` (no word filter is loaded, so a
message passes as with none configured). The research designs of the BCI, its components, the implanter's board and the circuit
implant are offered (DV384) and the techweb's circuit nodes unlock them; the BCI's assembly finishes into the BCI (DV570 retired).
Kept as tg has them: the circuit implant's core clears its implant before it reads its host when its shell goes; the bar overlay
keeps its overlays under the target but looks them up by the target's weak reference; the arrest console's component never fills its
options; the air alarm's vent component writes the external pressure into the internal bound; the messenger component reads the

Translated whole: `modules/mob/living/basic/lavaland/mining.dm` (the mining mob base with its crusher damage tracking and the
kinetic accelerator's bonus), the goliaths (`goliath/`: the goliath, its ancient and immortal kinds, the tentacles, the tentacle
attacks in a line and around it, the saddle and riding), the watchers (`watcher/`: the gaze, the beam, the overwatch, the
magmawing and icewing, their AI), the legions (`legion/`: the legion, its snow, dwarf and large kinds, the skulls and their
infesting of corpses, the legion monkey and the consumed corpses of `mob_spawn/corpses/mining_corpses.dm`), the hivelords and their
broods, the basilisks with their overheating, the brimdemons with their beam, the bileworms (spit, burrow, the vileworm), the
lobstrosities (charge, snip, the juveniles' growth and happiness) and the node drone with the ore vents' wave defence; the
whole `mining/equipment/monster_organs/` folder (the monster core base with its decay and the stabilizer, the regenerative core
with its in-hand heal and its implanted revival, the brimdust sac with the brimdust coating and its blast, the rush gland with the
lobster rush); `mining/equipment/kinetic_crusher/` (the crusher with the destabilizer, the mark and its detonation, the backstab,
`kinetic_crusher_trophies.dm`, `trophies_fauna.dm`, `trophies_misc.dm`: the retool kits and the crusher's skins). With them:
the biomes' fauna spawning and `mining_mob_respawner.dm` (a mob that dies respawns in an ash storm), the reskin component and atom
skins (`datums/components/reskinnable_atom.dm`, `__DEFINES/reskin.dm`) with every reskin site that waited for it (the detective's

`spawner.dm`, the explorer suit and hood with the goliath plates, the goliath cloak, the bone armour and the skull helmet, the
healing explorer's webbing, the changeling's brood. The trophies of the common fauna are crafted from their loot with the crusher as
the tool (`crafting/melee_weapon.dm:291-365`), as tg has them. Kept as tg has them: the retool kit's skin stays on the crusher
after the kit is pried off (the removal re-adds the reskin component with no skin, which the existing one absorbs); a lobstrosity
deleted mid-charge runtimes in the charge's cooldown with no owner; the icewing trophy freezes only the cold-resistant; the parry

## 241. Climb in, the MOD drill and the lizards' limbs (gaps FX1, D355)
The radial's Climb in (DV639) is offered on every live receiver of tg's drag of a player's own mob onto a thing that would take him in
or onto it: the exosuits and the other sealed vehicles, the netpod, the DNA scanner, the suit storage unit, the hypnochair, the implant
chair, the cryo cell (tg lets only an incapacitated carbon in by himself), the disposal bins and chutes, and Climb on for the
photocopier, the morgue and crematorium trays and a hacked MULEbot; each receiver answers quietly beside it
(`Infrastructure/ClimbIn.cs`, `dm_climb_in_label`), with tg's own drop checks (`ClimbIn.DropPasses`), and the choice sends tg's drag.
An open closet, locker, crate or body bag offers Climb in as tg's step onto its tile and its close (DV640). The drop receivers that take
no one in are not offered: the wall healer takes the user's hand, the DNA infuser only a dead creature, the transit tube station a
pod, the monkey recycler an unconscious monkey, the golf cart's rear buckles (Buckle), the walls, windows, airlocks and computers lean.
The MOD drill digs on its click held on the rock and not on a bump; the sphere's excavation keeps tg's bump (DV354; the client sends
the middle button's MouseDown, so the held dig runs while it is down; a module's click that waits runs detached, `_module.cs`
`on_special_click`). The lizards' limbs: the real client draws tg's lizard bodyparts on every lizard, a player turned lizard, a spawned
lizard and an ash walker with digitigrade legs, standing and lying, and his severed arm on the floor, from
`icons/mob/human/species/lizard/bodyparts.dmi` in his mutant colour (the server's appearance, lobby.md 10; screenshots in FX1's
report); no 2D limb is a human's, so the owner's "own limbs" is asked back as the 3D models' question (D338). Left:

## 236. Lavaland's fauna, second half, and the ice moon's mobs (gaps LV3, D339, D353)
Translated whole: `modules/mob/living/basic/lavaland/goldgrub/` (the goldgrub with its ore eating and spitting, its burrowing
through the rock, its girth that bounces kinetic shots, its taming and riding, the bluespace egg and the baby's growth),
`gutlunchers/` (the milk, warrior and grub gutlunchers, the udder's cream and salglu from gold and bluespace, the breeding with
the father's stats, the grub's growth, the warriors' pet commands and wall tearing, the food trough), `raptor/` whole (the
raptors and their seven colours with each colour's gift, the inheritance of stats and personality traits, the guaranteed
crossbreeds, the eggs and their hatching, the chick, youngling and adult stages, the happiness, the troughs, the baby, adult and
healer AI, the RaptorDex with its `RaptorDex` window), `mook/` (the mooks, workers, bards and chief, their leap, jump and ore
carrying, the village's material stand and landmark, their behaviour trees) and `modules/mob/living/basic/icemoon/` whole (the
ice demon with its teleport, slippery floors and afterimages, the ice whelp with its ice breath, eruption, sculptures and
cannibalism, the polar bears, the wolves with their taming, riding and pet commands). With them:
`modules/modular_computers/file_system/programs/raptordex.dm` (the NtOS RaptorDex and its `NtosRaptorDex` window),
`datums/components/riding/riding_minigame.dm` and the goldgrub's, raptor's, raptor healer's and chick's riding,
`datums/components/proficient_miner.dm`, `ai_listen_to_weather.dm`, `joint_damage.dm`, `datums/elements/ore_collecting.dm`,
`clever_girl.dm`, `raptor_food.dm` (with its sites: the bluespace crystal, the diamond ore, the four ash flora fruits, the meat
slabs), `datums/actions/mobs/fire_breath.dm` (the line, the cone and the mass fire), the conjure spell's `limit_summons`, the
targeting strategies `baby_raptor`, `injured_raptor`, `raptor_trough` and `unlit_bonfire`, `__DEFINES/basic_mobs.dm`'s
`gutlunch_count`, the living mob's own entry in its allies list (`living.dm:15`, a stub left from before `add_ally` was translated: a tamed wolf takes its tamer's allies), and the crusher's tail spike (`trophies_megafauna.dm:21-43`, the ice whelp's and the ash drake's trophy). The ice
demon cube, wolf ear, bear paw and raptor feather drop from their mobs (section 235's LV3 row). Lavaland's biomes and the cave
generator now place live goldgrubs and raptors, the mining base's ranch (`Lavaland.dmm`: three younglings, a chick, four troughs,
three RaptorDexes) is live, and the necrotech relic's summon, the dimensional anomaly's goldgrubs and the mook dust of the
flatpacks, the soulscythe, the bee smoker and the cursed katana come with them. Kept as tg has them: nothing sends
`COMSIG_ITEM_EATEN_BY_BASIC_MOB`, so `raptor_food`'s stats never reach a raptor, and `set_parents` sums the child's own (empty)
`foods_eaten` (`raptor_inheritance.dm:54`), so food never shapes a chick either; the happiness component's callback thresholds are percents (0 to 100) compared with the happiness as a fraction (`happiness.dm:101-112`), so a raptor's happiness never raises its attack or its growth (the RaptorDex's hearts, read from the blackboard, do fill); the gutluncher's udder callback calls
`update_overlays` whose list nobody applies, and a plain rebuild passes no volumes, so the full udder shows whenever the overlays are
rebuilt; the ice demon's slippery ice passes its phase-in period to the callback instead of `addtimer` and turns slippery a tick
after it appears; `clever_girl`'s `Detach` is declared on `/datum/element/cleaning` (`clever_girl.dm:9`), so the element never
unregisters its door signal. Left:

## 237. The megafauna and their loot (gaps LV4, D339, D353)
Translated whole: the legacy simple animal base (`simple_animal/simple_animal.dm`, `animal_defense.dm`, `damage_procs.dm`,
`hostile/hostile.dm`, the hostile mobs' unarmed attack in `_onclick/other_mobs.dm` and `controllers/subsystem/npcpool.dm`, which
drives their AI), `simple_animal/hostile/megafauna/` whole (the megafauna base with its devouring, telegraphed attacks, GPS signal
and crusher loot; the ash drake with its fire breath, meteors, lava swoop, flight shadow and lava arena, and the lesser drake;
Bubblegum with its blood warp, triple and hallucination charges and surround; the colossus with its spiral, random, shotgun,
alternating and final volleys, its projectile shield and the bolts that dust the dead; the hierophant with its chasers, cross
blasts, blinks, burst, arena walls and beacon; the legion with its skulls, charge, sentinels and splitting; the demonic frost miner
with its frost orbs, snowball guns, ice shotguns, enrage and prisms; the wendigo with its slam, scream, teleport and shockwaves;
the clockwork defender), `basic/boss/` (the boss base; the blood-drunk miner with its dash, rapid saw, kinetic accelerator,
dash attack, transforming saw, its guidance, hunter and doom kinds and its death effect; the Thing with its three phases, the
decimate, charge, tendrils, shriek and acid, the phase depleters and the aggro gates), the mob actions they use
(`datums/actions/mobs/`: fire_breath, meteors, lava_swoop, blood_warp, charge's triple and hallucination charges, projectileattack
whole, chase_target, create_legion_skull, create_legion_turrets with the legion sentinel, dash, teleport, ground_slam),
`mining_loot/megafauna/` whole (the drake's cloak, ghost sword, dragon's blood and lava staff; Bubblegum's H.E.C.K. suit, mayhem
bottle and the soulscythe; the colossus' vocal cords and anomalous crystals; the hierophant club with its blink and beacon; the
storm staff; the cleaving saw and the wildhunter knife; the frost miner's crystal, cursed boots and demonic jackhammer; the wendigo's
blood; the Thing's AI-uplink brain), the necropolis chests of the megafauna, `trophies_megafauna.dm` but the elites' trophies,
`necropolis_gate.dm` whole (the legion's gate), `bitrunning/util/virtual_megafauna.dm` (the virtual megafauna of the bitrunning
domains, whose six megafauna domains load now), the megafauna's and the bosses' shuttle-move notices, the boss vents' summoning, and with them the boss music, attack telegraph, wall smasher, knockback, projectile
shield and kill achievement elements, the dash weapon action, the vortex recall, the blooddrunk, mayhem, saw bleed and saw slowdown
status effects, the worn butchering component, the bubblegum gum. Kept as tg has them: the hierophant's damaging blast passes its
"penetrated" text into the armour check's silent slot; the legion's split never stops its own death, so each split legion dies at
once and only the last one drops the staff; the wendigo never starts its scream cooldown; the Thing's square tendrils always take
the big telegraph (its condition is a ternary of 2 or 1); the knockback element flips its shared distance on a negative throw; the
frost miner's second animate names its flags twice; the lava staff's reset of a lava turf reports a failure after it worked and starts
no cooldown; no one sends a boss vent's wave conclusion from its boss, so its node drone never comes; a crusher kill of the Thing,
the blood-drunk miner or the wendigo puts the trophy in the body's butchering (their crusher loot is not dropped at once). Fixed: the
turf references the drake's meteors and lava, the lava staff and the ice trail boots keep across a turf change, and the explosions'
turf lists (two colossus bolts on one wall in a tick); a hotspot made on a wall (a meteor's) no longer throws. Left:

Live: `modules/library/lib_machines.dm` whole (the visitor console with its archive search and the LibraryVisitor window; the book
management console with its inventory loaded from its area's books at mapload, the check-outs, the archive's search, the upload of the

scanned, its binding flick and its 4.1 s bind; the borrowbook datum; `library_updated`), `admin_only.dm` whole (the admins' console and
its book viewer, the LibraryAdmin and AdminBookViewer windows), `book.dm`'s DSM and IDC manuals (the DSMBook and IDCBook windows on tg's
paginated book), the barcode scanner's book scans, the library console's board and its screwdriver switch, `spells/spell_types/self/forcewall.dm`
whole (the wizard's forcewall, the cult's shield, the mime's invisible blockade) with the wizard's forcefield of `effects/forcefields.dm`,
`spells/spell_types/pointed/finger_guns.dm` whole, the emote invocations' `%PRONOUN_` (`spell.dm:372`, the mime's spells are the first to use them), and `SSdbcore`'s `Connect`, `IsConnected` and `Disconnect` up to the SQL pool. tg keeps
the library's archive in its SQL database (`SSdbcore`); the port has none (D76 keeps the host's own data, D332 keeps persistence out), so
`Connect` fails as tg's does with SQL off and the consoles take tg's own no-connection paths: the archive's windows show "Unable to
retrieve book listings", an upload or an archive print says "Connection to Archive has been severed. Aborting.", and the admins' console
closes its window. The scanner's and the binder's flicks play on their models' working surfaces (`models.json`). Kept as tg has it: the
news post without a news network says so and goes on into it; the check-out's period is `max(params, 1)`; the admins' window reads an
`author_ckey` field its console never sends. Left:

## 248. Ordnance and the small machines (gaps OR1, D339)
Live: `research/ordnance/` whole: the tank compressor (`tank_compressor.dm`: it pumps its input port's gas into an inserted tank up to
5000 kPa, keeps what the tank leaks in its buffer and records it, ten moles or more, as a gas record when the tank is destroyed or
ejected, absorbs a blast inside it, prints a record to a disk as a gaseous ordnance file with the experiments it fits; the
TankCompressor window), the tachyon-doppler array (`doppler_array.dm`: it records every blast in front of it within 150 tiles with the
factual and the theoretical radii and the exploding tank's reactions, states it, prints it to a disk; its red light while powered and
its rotation; the DopplerArray window), the scientific papers and their partners (`_scipaper.dm`, `scipaper_partner.dm`); the NT Frontier
app (`programs/frontier.dm`, the NtosScipaper window), the ordnance data files (`file_system/data.dm:36-87`) and the techweb's
`add_scientific_paper` (publishing completes the ordnance experiment and pays the science budget); `game/machinery/mass_driver.dm` with
`datums/wires/mass_driver.dm` (the testing range's, the chapel's, the disposals' and the shack's drivers with their launch flick) and
`computer/pod.dm` (the launch controls with the MassDriverControl window), and the mass driver button's controller
(`assembly/doorcontrol.dm:180-207`). Meta's compressor, doppler array, four drivers and four controls boot live. The models were already
in the catalog (the compressor's doors and hatch, the doppler's panel, the driver's launch flick). Kept as tg has it: the launch control's
`process` sleeps into its alarm (pod.dm:25, the port runs it detached as `set waitfor = FALSE` does); `add_scientific_paper` looks for
the experiment's type among the papers and so always completes it again; a paper's `0 in gains` never matches; the purchase message
reads its refund by text, so it says "refunding  points"; each `RefreshParts` adds the servos' range to the driver's again.
The anomaly refinery, the cores and their users, the gizmos and the pads came with gaps OR2 (section 254).

## 254. The anomaly cores, the gizmos and the pads (gaps OR2, D339)
Live: `research/anomaly/` whole: the anomaly refinery (`anomaly_refinery.dm`: a raw core and a transfer valve go in, the implosion opens
the valve inside it, catches the blast and makes the finished core when the blast reaches the radius the core's kind needs, which grows
from 4 to 20 tiles with the cores of that kind made, up to SSresearch's limit; the failed and timed-out tests eject both; the emag's
ejected live bomb; the bomb simulator; the AnomalyRefinery window), the raw cores (`raw_anomaly.dm`, the random raw core picking a kind)
and the anomaly cores (`anomaly_core.dm`: the signaler an anomaly carries on a random free frequency and code, neutralizing the
anomalies on its tile when its code reaches it, the gas analyzer's readout, and each kind's signal: the pyroclastic fire, the
gravitational pull, the flux zap, the bluespace blink, the vortex pull, the bioscrambler pulse, the hallucination pulse, the dimensional
turn of the tiles, the ectoplasmic haunt, the weather thunderbolt; the dimensional core's slapcrafting into the dimensional bomb core),
with `effects/anomalies/_anomalies.dm`'s cores (made with the anomaly, dropped by a neutralized one, an inert anomaly past the limit).
The signaler's `signal` is now a sleeping proc as in tg's slots (the bluespace core's blink sleeps), its callers detach it. The cores'
users made live: the reactive armour shell and its armours (`suits/reactive_armour.dm` whole but the fugitive hunters' psyker armour:
the teleport, incendiary, stealth, tesla, repulse, table, hallucinating, bioscrambling, barricade, ectoplasm and weather armours with
their EMP'd effects; `reactive_armour_dimensional_themes.dm` whole), the polymorphic field inverter (`belts/polymorph_belt.dm` whole:
the scan, the shapeshift with its channel and the status effect that follows an aging animal) and the perceptomatrix helm
(`head/perceptomatrix.dm` but the fishing difficulty: the core's traits, ear protection, mind anti-magic and screen colour, the
Hallucinate spell with its pancake blast). The users that were live and waited only for a core now get one: the wormhole projector,
the gravity gun, the anomalock heart, the Atlas spine, the MOD anomaly-locked modules, the dimensional bomb core, the weather tower,
the anomaly releaser, the ice demon's raw core. `research/gizmo/` whole (the controller, interfaces, puzzle with its code sequences,
wires, voice, the machines with the beyblade and toggle gizmos, the items, every gizmode and gizpulse) with
`datums/elements/moving_randomly.dm`; `game/machinery/quantum_pad.dm` whole (the multitool link, the keycard, the mapped links, the
charge and teleport with its beam flick, the USB circuit component), `computer/launchpad_control.dm` whole (the LaunchpadConsole
window, the USB component), `mechlaunchpad.dm` and `computer/mechlaunchpad.dm` whole (the MechpadConsole window, the mech launch by
supply pod), `ecto_sniffer.dm` with its wires. The sniffer's paranormal levels and the voice gizmos' words are mounted from tg's
`strings/`. Meta's refinery, its three random raw cores, its gizmo spawner and tucker tubes, its ecto sniffer and its orbital pad with
its console boot live (Meta maps no quantum pad and no launchpad console). Kept as tg has it: the random raw core picks among all raw
core subtypes, itself included (`raw_anomaly.dm:86`), and the next one picks again; the refinery window's giver column shows the
target tank's moles and its target column checks the giver's for the pressure (`AnomalyRefinery.jsx`); the refinery's stoppable timeout
timer keeps its id in a timer var, so `QDEL_NULL` only clears it and the timeout fires after the test ends; the food gizmode's
dispenser list repeats a key, so one of its two kinds is lost (`giz_filler.dm`), and it deletes its weakrefs instead of the food; the
thrower passes `GLOB.alldirs` as the direction when nothing is in reach (a runtime in tg too); the moo gizmo's loot table reads a
list's initial value, which is null (`gizcode.dm`); the copier's scan plays its sound on a datum, which plays nothing.

| `research/anomaly/anomaly_core.dm:27` | the core's `manual_suicide` (gibbing the suicider whose core gets its signal; a live-partial row, as the signaler's) | a suicide with a signaler core | M10+ (the suicide verb) | `modules/research/anomaly/anomaly_core.cs` |
